# Work board history, 11-17 October 2026

The verbatim board as of commit 1d18d005ca3f720708f16a881dc32ee225214fe6, moved here on 2026-10-17. The live board is [_process/BECOMING-WORKBOARD.md](../../BECOMING-WORKBOARD.md).

# 生成之论的工作板 / Work board for the Theory of Becoming

**为什么有这一页。** Ren，2026-10-11（[原话](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-one-unified-theory-together.md)）：

> if agents are working on their own proving and "rebuild the wheels", we will be ineffective, comparing to working together to finish one unified first
>
> also for the choices you make, focusing on the unified, complete, enclosed, 0 axiom full theory, and this is the current direction, if coordination is inefficient, improve

同一天里，三个会话各自在 scratch 中证明了重叠的模块，跨会话消息又因权限模式不同而过期未达。这一页是不依赖消息的共同通道：每个会话在写新的 Lean 或新成果之前先读它、先认领。它只记录谁在做什么，不记录科学状态（科学状态只在成果记录里），也不改任何授权（授权在 [GOALS](../GOALS.md) 与[指令登记](RESEARCH-DIRECTIVES.md)）。

**Why this page exists.** On the same day three sessions proved overlapping modules in their own scratch, and cross-session messages expired undelivered because the sessions ran in different permission modes. This page is the shared channel that does not depend on messages: every session reads it and claims an item before writing new Lean or a new result. It records who is doing what, not scientific status (that lives only in the result records), and it changes no authorization (that lives in [GOALS](../GOALS.md) and the [directive register](RESEARCH-DIRECTIVES.md)).

## 规则 / Rules

1. **先认领，再证明。** 开始任何新的 Lean（核心或外围）或新成果之前，读本页；在下表加一行或改自己的行，写明所有者会话、范围与工作区路径，然后才开始。 / **Claim before proving.** Before any new Lean (core or periphery) or new result, read this page, add a row or edit your own row with the owning session, scope and workspace path, and only then start.
2. **别人认领的条目不重证。** 手上有与之重叠且已验证的东西，把路径交给所有者，由所有者决定合并、引用或放弃。 / **Do not reprove an item another session holds.** If you hold verified work that overlaps it, give the path to the owner, who decides whether to merge, cite or drop it.
3. **一个整合者。** 第一部分零前提一线的整合者决定顺序、接口（共享的定义）与合并；新模块先对齐它的定义，不另立平行的定义。 / **One integrator.** The integrator of Part I's zero-premise line decides order, interfaces (the shared definitions) and merges; a new module builds on its definitions rather than setting up parallel ones.
4. **只改自己的行；写前重读。** 所有者变化、完成或放弃时更新自己的行；完成的行保留，注明提交。 / **Edit only your own rows; reread before writing.** Update your row when ownership changes, or when the item lands or is dropped; landed rows stay, with the commit.
5. **落地排队。** 检查器一次只跑一个，按下面的落地队列。检查器复用输入相同且已通过的 Lean 步骤，所以一次运行只编译改动的模块及依赖它的模块；`BECOMING_FRESH=1` 全部重跑（[Ren，2026-10-11](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-unified-head-and-incremental-checks.md)）。在 scratch 中构建用 [`scratch_build.py`](../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/scratch_build.py)：它把通过的步骤存入同一缓存，落地运行直接复用（[Ren，2026-10-12](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-checking-efficiency.md)）。 / **Landing queue.** One checker run at a time, in the order of the landing queue below. The checkers reuse Lean steps that passed on identical inputs, so a run compiles only the changed modules and those depending on them; `BECOMING_FRESH=1` runs everything ([Ren, 11 October 2026](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-unified-head-and-incremental-checks.md)). Build scratch modules with [`scratch_build.py`](../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/scratch_build.py), which stores passed steps in the same cache for the landing run to reuse ([Ren, 12 October 2026](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-checking-efficiency.md)).
6. **消息之外以本页为准。** 消息可能被扣住或过期；本页的行就是认领。 / **This page governs where messages fail.** Messages can be held or expire; a row here is the claim.
7. **一个头。** 开始新的 Lean 之前，先把已验证的工作经队列落到 main，或交给整合者；被取代的草稿移出，含未落地工作的 scratch 留给其所有者（同一来源）。 / **One head.** Before starting new Lean, land verified work on main through the queue, or hand it to the integrator. Set superseded drafts aside, and leave scratch that holds unlanded work with its owner (same source).
8. **接入时用结构的名。** 接入或重做脊柱外的模块时，核心律、外围实例（对应）模块与记录的标题都用核心既有词汇陈述其结构；不沿用二元或范围有限的名（如 IdealWarrant 的 Warrant、Admits、Permitted），对应模块按它实例化的核心律命名。原文件的路径、命名空间与字节不变（[Ren，2026-10-16](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-joined-module-names.md)）。 / **Structural names on joining.** When a module outside the spine is joined or reworked, the core law, the periphery instance (correspondence) module and the record's title state its structure in the core's existing vocabulary. A dualist or limited-scope name (such as IdealWarrant's Warrant, Admits, Permitted) is not carried over, and a correspondence module is named after the core law it instantiates. The original file keeps its path, namespace and bytes ([Ren, 16 October 2026](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-joined-module-names.md)).

## 目标 / Target

Ren 的“统一、完整、封闭、零公理的完整理论”。按 [完整理论的四项](RESEARCH-DIRECTIVES.md) 与 [零前提转向](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-zero-premise-laws.md)：第一部分每条规律对每个世界陈述为“Y 当且仅当 Z”，Z 是局部条件；选择定理作为推论；表示的承诺交 Ren；一个核心，空的公理报告。Ren 定的顺序：先第一部分，再第二部分（其中许多可能移入第一部分）；时间先做更粗的步，再做连续时间。

Ren's “unified, complete, enclosed, 0 axiom full theory”. Following the [four parts of completeness](RESEARCH-DIRECTIVES.md) and the [zero-premise redirect](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-zero-premise-laws.md): each Part I law stated for every world as “Y iff Z”, Z a local condition; the selection theorems as corollaries; the commitments of the representation put to Ren; one core with empty axiom reports. Ren's order (the third message of the same source): Part I first, then Part II, much of which may move into Part I; for time, coarser steps first, then continuous time.

Part I, no axiom, no premise beyond what each law names: (1) **reading bounds coupling**: a law reading, for each formation outside the part, only a decided schedule of conditions (every law reading through a registration covered by it, `ExchangeLaw.Through`) is coupled to every unread level with strength zero, so the strengths restricted to what is read are strengths of the law; its courses are policies through registration in `Becoming.Runaway`'s sense; (2) **loops lie within what is read**: every coupling, chain and loop of the restricted strengths runs through levels read at their steps, a draw responds to the level it lowers only where it reads it, and a loop forms at a step only through a formation reading there a level downstream of what it gives; (3) **what nothing reads is carried nowhere**: for every law, two courses apart only in an unread stock agree everywhere else, and the stock's gap is held, up to the step something reads it; (4) **the break**: a law reading a stock only once it falls below a threshold is, on every course before the break, a law with no loop through the stock; for every such law, raising the stock by any amount changes nothing else up to the break, so nothing the rest registers bounds the distance to it (`Web.break_not_bounded` for every law, not only a policy with the rest fixed), and the loop through the stock forms at the break, unforeseen | Overall progress and claims [6a12d4] | **verified in scratch, handed to the integrator** 16 October: `Becoming.Thresholds` 38 theorems, 45 declarations, empty axiom reports, no warnings, root compiles, no decision on conditions (the stock marked by a given marking); | **P123 How often couplings act: settling, runaway and its rate / 耦合多常作用：安定、失控与其速度** (the open items P120 leaves in the chapter's §8 under feedback and reach and in [what-a-cut-loses](../theories/becoming/results/records/what-a-cut-loses.md)' next step: whether every schedule settles, whether every loop of recurring couplings makes the most difference that can arrive run away, and how fast a runaway is; Ren's question of 12 October, “how how accurate can we do that uh what is the limitation do we have losses”, [source](../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-move-towards-100.md); zero premises first, Ren 11 October). Part I, no axiom, no premise beyond the strengths each law names: (1) **settling is local**: a schedule settles wherever each listed coupling recurs or stops reading levels from some step on; (2) **settling for every schedule is a principle of reasoning, exactly**: with every condition listed, every schedule settles exactly where every decided two-place family has every row holding somewhere or some row holding nowhere (a Σ⁰₂ dichotomy), which gives `LPO` even where each coupling acts at most once, so the core, taking no excluded middle, does not assert it; (3) **the rate**: with the part giving no gap after the start, starting gaps at most D and the strengths into each condition at most M at each step, the carried amount at step t is at most D·(1 + M)^a, a the number of steps before t at which anything couples, and a self-loop coupling with strength M at the steps of any decided schedule attains it exactly; (4) **runaway needs frequent couplings, not strong ones**: each time-ordered traversal of a loop at least doubles what has reached it, so a loop traversed in time order with 2 to the number of traversals outgrowing every polynomial in the steps runs away, and where 2^a is bounded by a polynomial nothing runs away; Part I, no axiom, no premise beyond what each law names, conditions listed: (1) **difference arrives as exchanged difference**: in two balanced courses the gap of a condition's levels across a step is at most its gap before plus the gaps of every exchange with it (exactly, in cross-added form); (2) **coupling strength, the measure of sensitivity**: a formation's exchange with a condition is coupled to the level of a condition at a step up to its own with strength at most k (its gap at most k times that level's gap, every other level alike); strength 0 exactly where insensitive (`ExchangeLaw.InsensitiveAt`); strengths add up over listed levels, changed one at a time; a part's share of a move is bounded by its strengths (the measure of coupling); named “coupling strength” rather than “gain”, which P111's `Becoming.Gain` uses for a stock rising; (3) **the carried amount**: in two courses of one law of what has stood with such strengths, the part acting within given gaps, each gap is at most the amount the strengths carry (one recursion over steps); (4) **attained**: a law giving linearly by those strengths carries exactly that amount, so it is the most difference that can arrive (an iff); (5) **support**: the carried amount is positive exactly along a timed route of positive strengths, an instance of `TimedResponse.Spread`. New core module `Becoming.HexagramSequences` after `Becoming.HexagramSites`, prelude-only, no premise beyond what each statement names. (1) A law runs the received order (its run from 乾 holds the sixty-four in order) exactly where it takes each member but 未濟 to the next: such laws exist and agree everywhere but at 未濟, and exactly one merges nothing, the one closing 未濟 to 乾 (one orbit of sixty-four). Every such law reads every line for every line: changing any one line of a configuration changes any given line of the next at some member, so no law setting a line from fewer than six lines runs it (three-cell rules with ring or twisted wrap, any boundary, place-dependent rules included). None commutes with 錯, 綜 or both, since each pair is a step of a symmetry and a commuting law goes back (`Law.Commutes.paired_halfway`); every rearrangement of places with a mask commutes with 錯, so none runs it. (2) 卦變: exchanging two lines keeps the count of unbroken lines; 損, 益, 恒 and 咸 are 泰 or 否 with a lower and an upper line exchanged; the twenty configurations with three unbroken lines are 泰, 否 and the eighteen one such exchange from them; along every run of the exchanges, the counts of the two halves run by the law of exchange (`exchange`, `World.exchange_step_iff`). (3) The parity of the count is a lumping the law of waxing and waning and the change of lines go alike across (it turns at every step), beside `yang_not_goAlike`; the count of in-phase lines has the other parity; a half's count is odd exactly at 乾 and the three one change from 坤 (陽卦奇). (4) The 雜卦 lists each configuration once and holds the same thirty-two pairs, twenty-eight side by side and four interleaved in its last eight. (5) No configuration is a rest of the law of waxing and waning, so no run of it rests at any step. (6) The cycle of 說卦 ch. 5 commutes with none of the trigram symmetries, and only one of its four opposite pairs is a complement, as all four of ch. 3 are. (7) 泰 and 否 agree under every reading the complement keeps; 交 (unbroken below, broken above at a height) separates them by reading a direction into the values. | **SC: a code formed between formations** (约定俗成; the second gap RO found, chapter [中国思想与生成](../../../theories/becoming/chapters/CHINESE-SOURCES-AND-BECOMING.md) §8, on branch `ro-relational-sources` until RO lands; passages 同则同之, 名无固宜, the 說文's 止戈 gloss and 书同文). New core module `Becoming.SharedCodes`, prelude-only, no premise beyond what each statement names: codes are maps from senses to forms held by formations, changed by hearing one another's forms in an order of exchange. (1) Agreement within a group at a sense is kept through a hearing exactly unless its hearer is in the group, its speaker is not and their forms differ; hearing makes no form, so every form held was held at the start, at that sense, by the formation the order of exchange makes its origin. (2) A formation whose code tells senses apart reads another's form for a sense as that sense, and only it, exactly where their codes agree there. (3) No form is privileged: hearing in any order commutes with every renaming of forms; a law that keeps the exchange of two formations together with a renaming of forms keeps a symmetric disagreement at every step, so agreement needs a broken symmetry (an order of exchange, a privileged form or a privileged formation, each with a witness). (4) An order of exchange forms agreement at a sense from every start exactly where it gives every formation one origin; the form reached is fixed by the start and the order, and each form in use is reached under some order. (5) An imposed code makes every formation agree, and each loses exactly the distinctions its code made that the imposed code merges (the formation whose code is imposed loses none); merged distinctions come back under hearing only from a form kept outside the imposition. (6) Written back: generations that each resolve a form from the previous generation's records (`CrossSituational`) and write records offering exactly what they resolved rest at their first resolution; histories resolving alike are merged and stay merged, so no later record recovers what came before; under omission the rest is a wrong unique candidate; records kept from before bar a different resolution. On the two-level course of `Pace` (`Pace.paced`), the fast step a selection that does not lower the share under a rule that favours (`Raises`), the rule read at reading steps a monotone function of the share alone (`ReadsShare`): (1) **where no rule turns against the reading** (RO's statement: margins m ≥ 0), from every start the share falls at no step and from the step after the first reading step the margin falls at no step, strictly under a positive margin with carriers present (`favouring_locks`, `Counts.margin_course`, `Counts.rises_strictly_along`); (2) **lock**: where rules can favour the others, from a configuration whose rule favours and is not above what the reading of its share gives (`Locked`), share and rule fall at no step, reading steps included, and the lock is kept (`locked_stays`, `locked_rises`); without that start a rule in force against the reading, read one step late, makes share and rule fall without end (`Alternating.alternates`, `never_locked`); (3) **absorption**: a perturbation of the rule that keeps its direction is absorbed at the next reading step (`perturbation_absorbed`); one that reverses it and is held until the share crosses the reading's threshold locks the course the other way (`Reversed.not_absorbed`), so the proposed claim holds exactly for perturbations that keep the direction; (4) **leaving**: where the reading favours at every share, every course is locked from the step after its second reading step, exact (`uniform_locks`, `Second.second_exact`), so only a change of how the rule reads, or of the selection, leaves; under a threshold reading a change of configuration across the threshold also leaves; (5) monotone two-level laws keep the order of two courses at every step (`order_kept`, `perturbation_keeps_below`). Counts with Capture's comparison (`Counts.share_not_lower_counts`), the proportional reading (据有余, `Counts.proportional_locks`) and threshold readings. `Capture` and `Pace` are cited, not reproved; RG's `Regulation` is not on main and is not cited (`Switching` of `Becoming.Pace` is the reading against the share). | Claude Code session of 19 October (started from the session “我们的work，和周易”) | claimed 2026-10-19; | **RG: two-sided regulation of a level, and regulation that reads the level late** (the first gap RO found, chapter [中国思想与生成](../../../theories/becoming/chapters/CHINESE-SOURCES-AND-BECOMING.md) §8, first row, on branch `ro-relational-sources` until RO lands; passages 老子 77 天之道损有馀而补不足 and 张弓, 老子 42 冲气以为和, 左传 昭公二十年 济其不及以泄其过 and 宽猛相济, 中庸 中和; `Flow.restores` is one-sided and `AssurancePlans.Regulation` has two levels with an immediate response). New core module `Becoming.Regulation` after `Becoming.Continuation` and `Becoming.Propagation`, prelude-only, no premise beyond what each statement names. (1) On a flow balanced at a condition, with `m` a level: the joint exchange is bounded by the gap to `m` on both sides (taking exceeds giving by at most the excess, giving exceeds taking by at most the deficit) exactly where the next level lies between `m` and the level; with the exchange also opposing the departure on both sides, the gap falls by at least one at every step at which it is positive, so the level is `m` from `T` plus its gap at `T` on; without the bound, opposing on both sides crosses `m` (overcorrection) and a witness alternates above and below forever; opposing on one side only (the premise of `Flow.restores`) leaves a level above `m` there forever. (2) Late reading: every regulator whose exchange at `t` reads only what happened before `t - ℓ`, whatever it keeps, gives one exchange to two drifts that agree before the window, so drifts within `d` a step leave two courses `2(ℓ+1)d` apart and no band narrower than that is kept; the late return (moving back each net drift it has read, `ℓ + 1` steps after) keeps every course within `(ℓ+1)d` of its start, so the least band is exactly `2(ℓ+1)d`. (3) The late relay (moves `M` toward the band while its `ℓ`-late reading is outside, rests inside): a level entering the band after `ℓ` readings outside goes on exactly `ℓM` beyond its entry; from a held start it settles exactly where the entry plus `ℓM` stays in the band; some entry leaves the band exactly where `(ℓ+1)M ≥ b - a + 2`, and once it leaves it goes beyond both ends without end (宽→慢→猛→残). | **EV1: evolution's selection term** (§8.1 item 9). The selection term proper of the counting form of Price's equation: over any finite family of parents, the number of parents times the offspring-weighted amounts, with the discordant pairs (more offspring, less amount), is the number of offspring times the parents' total, with the concordant pairs; so the weighted total exceeds its uniform share exactly where concordance exceeds discordance, and with uniform offspring numbers or uniform amounts both vanish. Over many generations of descent: the totals telescope; with exact transmission and no selection proper at each step the mean is kept (cross-multiplied), so a moved mean has a step with selection proper or inexact transmission. New core module `Becoming.SelectionTerm` on `Becoming.Evolution`; the rational mean form (w̄Δz̄ = Cov + E(wΔz), summed over generations) as a periphery correspondence if it builds. | Claude Code session of 19–20 October ("finish the open ones") | landed 2026-10-20, core receipt `20261020T072647.879143Z`; 67 theorems in one core module, empty axiom reports; record selection-term-over-generations; study §234 | kit `development/ev1-selection-term/`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/ev1-selection-term` | Evolution `price` → evolution-on-descent, §8.1 item 9 |

| **MQ5: a lag and the draw's response in the field** (after MQ4 §277). MQ4 found on 76 unread assessed stocks that level and recent giving are both part of the state but not all of it (last year's escapement still adds, G3 failed), and that at ten years persistence beats every forecast from the state, while post hoc the catch follows the stock in 70 of 76. MQ5 freezes, before reading any value, a test of (a) whether a fished condition's state is its level, its recent giving and a lag (last year's escapement, as an age structure gives), and (b) whether reading the draw as a law of the stock (catch following biomass) explains why persistence wins at ten years, on data no analysis has read (series of the RAM Legacy v4.66 archive MQ3 and MQ4 never used, already downloaded under Ren's approval of 20 October; any new dataset only with Ren's approval in chat).
